## THE VOLUME 07 CLOSE, AND ITS REVIEW REPAIR PASS — the authoritative block for the current phase, and it wins over everything above it

**Current phase: `volume-08-outline` (NOT RUN. IT IS A VOLUME OUTLINE PHASE AND NOT A BATCH. It writes `outline/volume-08.md`, the first Volume 08 batch's cards and no prose, and it is the only phase in this repository after a close phase that is allowed to decide. `workspace/volume-08/outline/PROMPT.md` EXISTS AND IS THE NEXT PHASE. There is no Chapter 0351 to write and this phase does not write one.)**

**Volume 07 is finished on day 350 and has been since Batch 0005. The close phase has since run and its record is `state/volume-07-close.md`, which is the page a writer reads before planning anything, and a review ran on that phase and returned six blocking defects, five wrong published figures and two minor items, and a review repair pass fixed all of them. The repairs are at `state/volume-07-close.md` §9 and this block is the state layer's half of them. No day moved, no weekday moved, no beat moved, no thread opened or closed, no decision of record was withdrawn, `outline/ending.md` was not opened, and no new final enemy exists.**

**THE FOUR THINGS THE REPAIR PASS ACTUALLY DID, because a state file that reports a repair is worth nothing unless the repair happened, and three of the six blocking defects were a record describing work it had not done.**

1. **`bible/power-system.md` §33B's day table for days 351 to 400 was wrong on twenty-four of its fifty rows and is repaired.** The right-hand block began `0376 | 376 | Tuesday` where day 376 is a Saturday, so every row 376 to 399 was one step out and row 0400 snapped back to a correct Tuesday and the block was discontinuous with itself. **All fifty rows now recompute from day 1 at zero mismatches. BM numbers were right in all fifty and were not touched; the left-hand block for 351 to 375 was right in all twenty-five and was not touched; §33A's table for 301 to 350 re-checks at zero mismatches.** §33B item 3's prose also said the forty-ninth day of the Bare Month is a Sunday, and the forty-ninth is day 364, which is a Monday; that sentence is corrected and the thirty-fifth-is-Monday and eighty-fifth-is-Tuesday beside it were right. **Day 351 is the thirty-sixth of the Bare Month and a Tuesday; day 400 is the eighty-fifth and a Tuesday; the midpoint day 375 is a Friday and the sixtieth; and the Bare Month still has no stated length and Volume 08 may not end it.**
2. **Five figures in the Volume 08 decisions block in `outline/series.md` were wrong and are corrected with the definition published, which that block did not have.** It said 329 chapter files and there are **350**, in three places. *Commons* is **41 in 19 files either case, 22 in 12 capitalised, 19 in 8 lower-case**, and not *41 in 8*. *Player* is **6 in 6 files** and *players* **8 in 6**, and the *16* was the two added together. ***labor* was a false zero — this manuscript spells it *labour* and stands 58 times in 37 files — so the argument that the struck crop-and-water line turned on an unused noun is withdrawn, and the strike stands on untouched ground: this book has no crop-and-water crisis, only a channel dry since the hundred and ninety-sixth day of the flood, a man of about sixty-three farming two grounds, and a man of about sixty-four digging somebody else's ditch nine hundred paces off and is not paid.** *Earthside* is **1 in 1 file** word-bounded and case-sensitive, **2 in 2** either case. *Redroot* 65 in 30, *Ivenn* 46 in 26, *players* 8 and all nineteen Volume 07 zeros reproduce exactly. **The Volume 08 line stays struck and stays replaced exactly as written; a false zero is corrected and a decision does not move.**
3. **The four decisions' cast paragraph merged two men and does not any more.** It said the man of about thirty-nine who trades on a board *is a return* and refused a page with his name at the top of it *in Chapter 0334*, and both belong to **the man of about fifty-seven**, who has answered a return four times a year and who refuses in `chapters/volume-07/chapter-0334.md:19` and `:27`. **The board-trader's refusal is Chapter 0340.** Repaired by naming 0340 and dropping the descriptor, not by adding a clarification.
4. **`chapters/volume-07/chapter-0309.md:41` said *thirty-seven* where the settled age is thirty-nine, and now says thirty-nine.** One word, asserted against the string, nothing else in the line or the chapter moved. **`man of about thirty-seven` is now 6 in 1 file, all six in `chapters/volume-06/chapter-0293.md`, and that man is a real person on a page and is not the board-trader: he carries a sack, does not open it, and stands in eight kitchens working out whether four is a number anybody should be given tonight. What has been withdrawn is the board, the market and the refusal, which were never his.** `man of about thirty-nine` is **133 in 69 files** word-bounded, 132 in 68 before the repair, and the record's original figure of *150 in 75* matched no instrument — the nearest is 156 in 76, an unbounded search that returns the 23 instances of *woman of about thirty-nine* inside it, and its Volume 05 file count was 26 where there are 19.

**AND THE FOUR FAULTS THAT ARE STILL UNREPAIRED AND ARE DEBTS, NOT WORK FOR THE NEXT PHASE, at `state/volume-07-close.md` §7.4 and §9.7.** Nineteen stray closing quotation marks in Chapters 0281 to 0290. **`chapter-0341.md:149` reads *the word on the slate has sixteen days left on it* and the figure is one — assert `the word on the slate has sixteen days left on it` and replace with `the word on the slate has one day left on it`, because the word came off on day 342 and 342 − 341 = 1.** A forty-word near-duplicate across the 0254 and 0262 boundary. An unmarked first-person paragraph at `chapters/volume-04/chapter-0175.md:47` — a paragraph carrying neither a quotation mark nor a bold mark, in the first person, with no speech open around it. **The pointer was wrong twice and both wrong numbers are recorded here: the inherited pointer says `:65`, which is a marked speech and is not the fault, and the pointer the outline phase published in its place said `:139`, which is interior to the speech opened at line 137 and closed at line 141 and is not the fault either, so the instrument is a paragraph with neither marker in the first person that is not inside a speech, and this file has exactly two such candidates in the first person, line 47 and line 139, and 47 is the debt.** **A repair pass that quietly takes a routed item to save itself a line is how those nineteen marks became a four-volume debt.**

**AND THE TWO FIGURES A LATER WRITER IS STILL TOLDRONG.** **The word on the register-keeper's second slate is on a page once in Chapter 0310, in narration twice in Chapter 0342, in no mouth at all, and at zero in the other forty-seven files of the volume, and no file the next phase produces may print it. It was on that slate for thirty-two days — 342 − 310 = 32 — and `outline/volume-07.md` §13's *sixteen* is corrected to thirty-two in the outline's own words, and any figure that comes out at exactly half of the right one is a subtraction taken across the wrong boundary.** The count of this city's silences is derived, on no page, in no mouth, and is **240 at day 340 and 250 at day 350** and is printed in no chapter.

**THE NEXT PHASE IS `workspace/volume-08/outline/PROMPT.md` AND IT IS A VOLUME OUTLINE PHASE AND NOT A BATCH.** It writes no chapter. ## THE VOLUME 08 OUTLINE — the authoritative block for the current phase, and it wins over everything above it

**Current phase: `volume-08-batch-0001` (NOT RUN. IT IS A BATCH AND IT WRITES CHAPTERS 0351 TO 0360. `workspace/volume-08/batch-0001/PROMPT.md` EXISTS AND IS THE NEXT PHASE.)**

**The phase that wrote this block was an outline phase and it wrote no chapter. Its plan is `outline/volume-08.md` and its first ten cards are `outline/batches/volume-08-batch-0001.md`, and its four decisions are in `outline/series.md` under *DECISIONS OF RECORD, VOLUME 08 — ADDED AND AMENDED BY THE VOLUME 08 OUTLINE PHASE*, in that file's own words. `state/volume-07-close.md` is still the volume's inheritance and this block does not displace it; where this block and that file disagree about a day, a figure or a name, this block is the later word and a chapter outranks both.**

**WHAT VOLUME 08 IS, IN THE ONE SENTENCE THE FOUR INHERITED DECISIONS GAVE IT.** A journey has to be made in this flood before the season turns, and the only instrument this world has for a journey is four people who have not left in two hundred and five days **at day 350, which is two hundred and six at day 351 and two hundred and fifty-five at day 400, all three measured from day 145 and not from the start of the flood**, and there is no form in four hundred miles for saying who is going on it, and the page that would carry them has a column for what they are going to do and none at all for the person. **Its central pressure is a journey nobody can write down. Its antagonist is a method and not a form: a quarterly return with a heading and four places under it and no place for the person, and a man who fills a column in from what he is given, and nobody asks him. Its midpoint is day 375, a Friday, the sixtieth day of the Bare Month, and somebody asks one of the four whether it will go for the first time in this flood, and the asking is the event. Its climax is day 393, a Tuesday, the seventy-eighth, where a page with a person's name at the top of it goes out on a date and the man of about thirty-nine is right that the day after that there is a column and the page goes anyway. Its resolution is that the journey is made and is not written down, and the register carries a line with a day on it, day 391, and nothing under the day. Its new question is asked on day 396 and is not answered. Its last image is day 400, a Tuesday, the eighty-fifth, and it is a fence.**

**THE FOUR NEW DECISIONS, briefly, and the file has them at length.** **(FIVE) The volume's own name is a man's answer, spoken once, in a room, in daylight, with a date on the door, on day 357, and it is written down in no file — not in this one, not in the outline, not in the batch record, not in the prompt.** The woman of about twenty-nine who keeps a public register asks the man of about thirty-eight at the salt wharf what the column is for; he has never been asked it in nine years; he answers in four to eight words and what he says is the name of the volume, and no chapter may state that, and no file may record the words. **(SIX) The four places under the heading are a district, a population, a rota and a share, and the fourth is a share, and a share is a household's, and a household's days are not a rota — and the household is a woman of about fifty-two, new, at zero across Volume 07, and she refuses the fourth place out loud in about four seconds on day 356, and she is NOT the woman of Chapter 0266 and that sentence is not in her mouth.** **(SEVEN) Three go, the page a clerk's copy was made from is not fetched, and the town four hundred miles inland is not entered.** **(EIGHT) The new question is about the fence and is asked on day 396 by the woman of about sixty-nine, who asked the last one on day 346, and neither question is answered and the rhyme is deliberate.**

**THE CALENDAR, BUILT FROM DAY 1 AND NOT FROM ANY MAP, and this is the thing to check first.** Day 1 is a Tuesday. **Days 351 to 400, one chapter to one day, no double day, the form is *the Nth day of the Bare Month*, the Nth being day less 315. Day 351 is the thirty-sixth and a Tuesday. Day 400 is the eighty-fifth and a Tuesday. Day 375 is a Friday and the sixtieth. Day 393 is a Tuesday and the seventy-eighth. Day 395 is a Thursday and the eightieth. Day 396 is a Friday and the eighty-first. Day 353 is a Thursday and the thirty-eighth.** The run was built from day 1 being a Tuesday and from nothing else, and was then validated by reproducing `bible/power-system.md` §33A item 4's table for days 301 to 350 and §33B item 3's repaired table for days 351 to 400 out of the same two figures, at **zero mismatches on all one hundred rows**. The tables were not copied and were not read until after the run existed. **A weekday on a page outranks a weekday in a plan, and a day map is a plan.**

**THE BARE MONTH STILL HAS NO STATED LENGTH AND DOES NOT END IN THIS VOLUME.** A chapter may write the thirty-sixth to the eighty-fifth and **may not write a day after the eighty-fifth, may not say the month is nearly over, may not say what it is a month of, may not print *eighty-five days* or *three months* or *since the month began* as a figure, and may not say what it does at its end, how many days it has, or whether it is long or short.** No chapter of Volume 08 may use an ordinal for a month. **A seventh form exists the moment the end of the Bare Month is on a page and it comes on the way the sixth one did: a room, in daylight, with a date on the door, by a person, who says the cost out loud first and is refused before she does it, and who then declines to say how long the new month is.**

**THE SEVEN NOTICES OF VOLUME 08, all four days, all unsigned, all with no name at the top, all to a place and not to a house: notice on 351 for a room on 355; 354 for 358; 359 for 363; 362 for 366; 366 for 370; 371 for 375; 389 for 393.** **A notice on any other day is a three-day instrument and a three-day instrument arrives warm.** Chapter 0351 is the volume's first page and carries the first one.

**AND THE ONE BIBLE THING THIS PHASE AMENDED, which `state/volume-07-close.md` §7.3 handed to whoever next writes §33A.** **`bible/power-system.md` §33A item 7's register of notices named six where Volume 07's chapters carry seven. It is amended to the seven, and the one it omitted is the day-305 notice for the room on day 309, which is the one that put the woman of about sixty-nine on a page, and the arithmetic closes at 4 + 1 = 5 and 2 + 5 = 7. The amendment is in item 7 and in no other section of §33A or of §33B, and no batch may make, amend or extend any of it.**

**AND THE FOUR FAULTS STILL UNREPAIRED, carried as debts and not as work, all four re-measured by this phase on the files and all four still there.** Nineteen stray closing quotation marks in Chapters 0281 to 0290, at `0281:37,65,81`, `0282:31,39,57`, `0283:47,53`, `0284:13,39`, `0285:39`, `0286:63`, `0287:25,41`, `0288:43`, `0289:31,49`, `0290:9,25`. **`chapters/volume-07/chapter-0341.md:149` says *the word on the slate has sixteen days left on it* where the figure is one — assert that string and replace it with *the word on the slate has one day left on it*, because the word came off on day 342 and 342 − 341 = 1.** A forty-word near-duplicate between `chapters/volume-06/chapter-0254.md` and `chapters/volume-06/chapter-0262.md`, which is a batch boundary and not a volume boundary. An unmarked first-person paragraph at `chapters/volume-04/chapter-0175.md:47` — a paragraph carrying neither a quotation mark nor a bold mark, in the first person, with no speech open around it. **The pointer was wrong twice and both wrong numbers are recorded here: the inherited pointer says `:65`, which is a marked speech and is not the fault, and the pointer the outline phase published in its place said `:139`, which is interior to the speech opened at line 137 and closed at line 141 and is not the fault either, so the instrument is a paragraph with neither marker in the first person that is not inside a speech, and this file has exactly two such candidates in the first person, line 47 and line 139, and 47 is the debt.** **A repair pass that quietly takes a routed item to save itself a line is how nineteen quotation marks became a four-volume debt, and the outline phase that wrote this block carried all four and fixed none.**

**AND THE FIGURES THE NEXT BATCH INHERITS, measured by this phase with the definition beside each one, and every one of them reproduces a published figure exactly.** Bolding, definition: heading line included, `---` rules counted, bold marks counted as nothing, file split on whitespace — **Volume 07's last ten: 8.4, 10.6, 9.5, 10.3, 10.7, 11.6, 9.3, 8.4, 8.6, 7.0 per cent, range 7.0 to 11.6, mean 9.4, forty-nine spans; all fifty files 7.0 to 25.4, mean 13.5, three hundred and eleven spans, and the longest bolded turn in the fifty is NINETY-FIVE WORDS in Chapter 0307, which is at the hard ceiling of ninety-five and not over it.** Exchange, definition: a plain speech is a paragraph carrying a quotation mark and no bold mark, answered when the very next non-blank paragraph carries a quotation mark and a bold mark — **Volume 07: 329 plain, 121 answered, thirty-six point eight per cent; last ten 134 and 27 at twenty point one.** Chorus head, every occurrence of the string *about four people in*, heading lines and `---` rules removed — **79 in 445 paragraphs on the last ten, one in five point six, at zero in Chapters 0345 and 0347 and in no other file of the ten.** Word-run, sequential token match with each file's own lead paragraph subtracted — **top 56 between Chapters 0348 and 0350, then 55, 46, 44, 42 and 41, and all six reproduce `state/volume-07-close.md` §7.1 item 4 exactly.** Descriptor map, sentence level, word-bounded, hyphen inside the boundary — **Volume 07 carries twenty-nine distinct descriptors in 1,634 paragraphs and seventy-eight stand across the 350 files; `man of about thirty-nine` is 133 in 69 files, which is the close record's settled figure, and `man of about fifty-seven` is 61 in 19 in Volume 07 alone.** **A mean is a consequence and not a plan, and no volume may read its own mean as a quality figure and no review may fail a volume on one.**

**AND THE TWO DESCRIPTORS VOLUME 08 ADDS, each measured at ZERO across Volume 07's fifty files before it was taken, with the 350-file figure beside it because a low 350-file figure is the best guard against the merge a word-bounded count cannot see: a man of about fifty-nine, 1 in 1 file across three hundred and fifty, who walks the four-hundred-mile road and who came up it in the ninth year of this flood and found the bar on a gate rotted through and put in a bar and hung a hurdle on it, and is nobody's man and is the last image's man; and a woman of about fifty-two, 7 in 6 files, who keeps a household of about nine and is asked for her days and refuses the fourth place.** And the two the volume deliberately avoids: *man of about forty-four* is at zero in Volume 07 but stands at 162 in 85 across the manuscript, and **a descriptor at zero in one volume and enormous in the six before it is a descriptor with a person attached to it that no page has ever named, and that is the exact shape of the fault the two new rules about instruments were written for.**

**AND THE OBJECT INVENTORY IS PUBLISHED, because a set of objects counted in two chapters is counted by no check in this repository and the volume's last image is an object.** The figures that hold across Volume 08 and that no two chapters may print differently are: **one return, with a heading and four places under it, a district, a population, a rota and a share, and no place for the person; the fence at sixteen posts cut, nine in the ground on day 348, four more on day 350 making thirteen, the last three on day 353, and eleven withies, and the four feet is not a measurement and is not in the table because nobody took it; seven notices; a party of four of whom three go and one said no; three barrows; about four hundred and forty on eleven miles of flats, and the figure the man of about thirty-eight has in his head is on no page and is printed nowhere; one register, one second slate with a date on it and nothing under it, and one line entered on day 391 with a day on it and nothing under the day; one folded sheet of road with about nine places on it; nine boards and a bare wall; one gate with two bars and one roll entry.** Full table with the chapters that name each at `outline/volume-08.md` §20.10.

**THE NEXT PHASE IS `workspace/volume-08/batch-0001/PROMPT.md` AND IT IS A BATCH AND NOT AN OUTLINE. It writes Chapters 0351 to 0360, ten chapters, days 351 to 360, one chapter to one day, from a Tuesday to a Thursday. It is told not to decide what the volume is, not to resolve anything listed as live, not to invent a person, and not to record the words the man of about thirty-eight says in Chapter 0357 in any file it produces, including its own record.**

## THE VOLUME 08 OUTLINE — REVIEW REPAIR PASS — the authoritative block, and it wins over every block above it

**Current phase: `volume-08-batch-0001` (NOT RUN. IT IS A BATCH AND IT WRITES CHAPTERS 0351 TO 0360. `workspace/volume-08/batch-0001/PROMPT.md` EXISTS AND IS STILL THE NEXT PHASE, AND THIS PASS CREATED NO SECOND PROMPT, because a repair pass that forks the hand-off is how two phases get written into the same ten days.)**

**WHAT THIS PASS WAS. A review of the Volume 08 outline phase, published at `logs/outline.review.log`, returned eleven items. This pass re-measured every one of them on the files before touching anything, and it did not take the review's word for a single figure. It repaired eight, rejected two as measurements taken on a narrower instrument than the file publishes, and did not act on one because it is the workflow's file and not this repository's. No chapter was written, no card's beat moved, no day or weekday moved, no outline figure that was right moved, `outline/ending.md` was not opened, no new final enemy exists, and the four decisions of record and the volume's plan are unchanged.**

**THE EIGHT REPAIRED, and every one is a figure or a pointer, and none of them is a story.**

1. **A FALSE ZERO, AND THE FAULT CLASS IS A SCOPE WIDENED WITHOUT THE FIGURE CHANGING. THE WORD ON THE SECOND SLATE.** Four files published it as "at 2 across all three hundred and fifty chapter files." **Measured: 2 is right for Volume 07's fifty files and wrong for three hundred and fifty, where the lower-case form stands at 115 and the capitalised at 1, the hundred and thirteen extra being fifty in Volume 01, thirty-four in Volume 02 and twenty-nine in Volume 03 and every one of them an injury on a person and none of them the mark in the orchard.** Repaired at `outline/series.md`, `outline/volume-08.md` §16, `outline/batches/volume-08-batch-0001.md`, `state/continuity.md`, `state/open-threads.md` and `workspace/volume-08/batch-0001/PROMPT.md` — **six sites, four of them cited by the review and two of them found by this pass in the same claim in two files the review did not cite.** **A prohibition on a common noun that stands a hundred and thirteen times in three earlier volumes is not a prohibition a writer can apply, so the repair publishes both figures and the second sense at the point where the figure is given, and a chapter may now use the common noun about a person without breaching anything.**

2. **THE SAME FAULT CLASS, THE SAME WEEK. THE WORD *DELEGATION*.** Two files published it as "at zero across all three hundred and fifty files" and one of them as "there is no word for what they are in this language." **Measured: 17 in four files, six in `chapters/volume-03/chapter-0145.md`, six in `chapters/volume-04/chapter-0163.md`, four in `chapter-0189.md`, one in `chapter-0188.md`, and all seventeen are these same four people, because Chapter 0145 is the chapter in which this manuscript named them and it named them this.** It is at zero in Volume 07's fifty files, which is the scope `state/volume-07-close.md` §2 and §7 use and which is correct. **The decision did not move and the decision is not what was wrong: the four are still four people and not a company and not a body and no new word may be invented. What moved is the reason. The prohibition this volume carries is not that the language lacks the word; it is that the four are not named in this volume and no chapter of it puts the word into a mouth that has not said it.** Repaired at `outline/volume-08.md` §6 and `outline/batches/volume-08-batch-0001.md` card 0359.

3. **A NOTICE ARITHMETIC WRONG TWICE, IN THE CLASS §33A ITEM 8 CALLS THE LARGEST IN THIS MANUSCRIPT.** Card 0360 said the day after 360 a notice goes for a room on 366 and the day after that one for a room on 375, and that the hinge is four chapters past the batch. **Measured: tomorrow is 361, 361 → 366 is five days and 362 → 375 is thirteen, and the register is 362 → 366 and 371 → 375, so the card as written produced a three-day instrument that arrives warm and an eighth notice; and the hinge is Chapter 0375 against a batch ending at 0360, which is fifteen chapters and not four.** Repaired in the card against §14.4's table, and the last notice in the batch is 359's, whose room is 363.

4. **A FALSE ZERO OF THE SAME CLASS AGAIN, AND THE SAME PHASE HAD IT RIGHT IN ANOTHER FILE. THE WORD *QUEST*.** The prompt published it at zero across three hundred and fifty files; **measured: 2 in 2 files, `chapters/volume-01/chapter-0019.md:169` in a man's mouth and `chapters/volume-01/chapter-0020.md:165` in narration, both in italics, both about a thing in a wall, and at zero for six volumes.** **`outline/volume-08.md` §20 already published the correct figure, so this was one file contradicting another inside the same phase.** Repaired in the prompt. **And while in the file: the outline's own correct sentence said "both in a character's mouth" and the second of the two is in narration, which is now also repaired.**

5. **A DEBT POINTER THAT WAS WRONG TWICE, THE SECOND TIME WITH AN INSTRUMENT BOLTED TO IT.** The inherited pointer for the unmarked first-person paragraph was `chapter-0175.md:65`, which is a marked speech. **The outline phase moved it to `:139` and published a justification for it — that `:139` is a first-person narration paragraph inside the bolded turn running from 137 to 141. That description is of correct prose: `:139` is a continuation inside the speech opened at `:137` with `"**` and closed at `:141` with `**"`, and a continuation inside a speech is properly unmarked. The instrument excluded nothing; it described a paragraph that is not the fault.** **Measured: this file has exactly two paragraphs carrying neither speaker marker in the first person, and they are `:47` and `:139`, and the instrument — neither marker, first person, not inside a speech — leaves `:47`, which is the debt. Repaired at all six sites, with both wrong line numbers recorded, because a pointer with a wrong instrument on it is worse than a pointer with no instrument at all.** The debt is unpaid and is still owed to a Volume 07 review-repair pass.

6. **A BIBLE SECTION CARRYING TWO REGISTERS AT ONCE, AND ONLY ONE OF THEM ROUTED.** `bible/power-system.md` §33A item 7's own paragraph said six and listed six, and the amendment appended beneath it said seven and listed seven. **A reader who took the first paragraph took six, and the review is right that this is a second stale register and not a first.** **Repaired in place, in the one section the Volume 07 close phase handed to the Volume 08 outline phase, and the register is now the seven at both places.** The omission was the day-305 notice for the room on day 309, and the arithmetic closes at 4 + 1 = 5 and 2 + 5 = 7. **`bible/power-system.md` §33B item 8a still says §33A item 7 IS SHORT BY ONE and is still routed, and this pass did not amend it, because the phase that amended §33A was forbidden to amend §33B and a repair pass does not widen its own remit to save itself a line.**

7. **A DESCRIPTOR FIGURE LIFTED FROM THE WRONG VOLUME AND RELABELLED.** Card 0354 published the stall man as "on a page in twenty-seven paragraphs across eight chapters of Volume 07 and in two hundred of this manuscript's chapters." **Measured on word-bounded, case-sensitive `man of about thirty-four`: 17 occurrences in 16 paragraphs across 10 chapters of Volume 07, and 283 in 276 paragraphs across 108 chapters of the manuscript. The twenty-seven paragraphs across eight chapters is Volume 06's figure and is correctly filed at `outline/volume-07.md` §6.4's cast row — it was lifted and relabelled, not invented.** Repaired in the card, with the string the figures are measured on published beside them, and with the longer descriptor carrying the trade in it, 237 in 100, given so that a writer who measures the other string is not left to guess which one was meant.

**THE TWO THIS PASS REJECTED, and it publishes both rejections because a repair pass that silently drops a routed finding is the fault this repository has been repairing for four volumes.**

9. **THE DESCRIPTOR-MAP COUNTS OF TWENTY-NINE AND SEVENTY-EIGHT ARE CORRECT AND WERE NOT TOUCHED.** The review returned twenty-eight and seventy-seven against `state/current.md`'s "twenty-nine distinct descriptors in 1,634 paragraphs and seventy-eight stand across the 350 files." **Measured on the instrument published in the very same sentence — head of man, woman, person, girl or boy, then *of about*, then a spelled-out number, sentence level, word-bounded, hyphen inside the boundary — the result is twenty-nine and seventy-eight, exact.** **The review's twenty-eight and seventy-seven is the four-head result, and the head it dropped is *person*, which contributes exactly one descriptor to Volume 07, `person of about twenty-one`, and one to the 350 files.** With the head list as published the file is right; with one head removed it is wrong; **a check that returns a fault where there is none is the same failure as a check that returns zero where there is one, and in a repository where a prohibition binds every later writer it is the more dangerous of the two, because it makes a writer distrust a figure that is true.** Nothing was changed here and the figures stand.

# VOLUME 08, BATCH 0001 — Chapters 0351 to 0360, days 351 to 360

**This block wins over everything above it. It is the authoritative block for the current phase. Its record is `state/batch-summaries/volume-08-batch-0001.md` and its chapters are `chapters/volume-08/chapter-0351.md` through `chapter-0360.md`. The block it displaces is the Volume 08 outline repair pass, and `state/volume-07-close.md` is Volume 07's inheritance and is not displaced. Where this file and a chapter disagree about a day, a name, a descriptor or a figure, the chapter is right.**

**WHAT IS ON A PAGE NOW, and it is ten chapters and it is finished prose and it is not a plan.** A form with a heading and four places under it came up the road in a satchel on the thirty-sixth day of that month and was put on a table in a room over a market and was read out in the local language in daylight with a date on the door and about nine people in it on the fortieth, and the four places are **a district, a population, a rota and a share**. The first has nowhere to start and the third is empty on purpose and the second is a number one man keeps in his own head, and the fourth is a share and a share is a household's. **A woman of about fifty-two refused the fourth place out loud in about four seconds on a step on the forty-first day of that month, in front of a woman with a slate, and named the cost first, and did not define the word, and her second handle is a door she is not asked to open.** A man of about thirty-eight on the boards of a salt wharf was asked what the column is for on the forty-second day of that month, in a room, in daylight, with a date on the door, by the woman of about twenty-nine who keeps a public register, and he answered, out loud, once, in the local language, in seven words, and **what he said is the name of this volume and it is on a page in Chapter 0357 and in no file in this repository, and this block does not record it, and the batch record does not record it, and the next prompt does not record it.** A fence was finished on the thirty-eighth day of that month, a Thursday, at **sixteen willow posts, nine in the ground on the thirty-third day of that month, four more on the thirty-fifth making thirteen, the last three on the thirty-eighth, and eleven withies bound with the same cord**, by a man nobody sent, who has not been asked and is not going to be. Three of the four who have not left are on a page as three people who said yes in their own words, and the fourth is on a page as a woman who said no before anybody asked her, and **nobody in these ten chapters counted the days since, at any value, and nobody was asked whether it will go.**

**THE FIGURES, and the definitions travel with them, and the record is where they are measured.** The share of words inside bold marks, heading line included, `---` rules counted, bold marks counted as nothing, file split on whitespace: **13.1, 10.1, 5.2, 10.7, 13.1, 14.0, 8.2, 8.0, 10.0 and 9.4, a range of 5.2 to 14.0 and a mean of 10.2 unweighted and 10.4 weighted by words**, with each chapter's subject beside the figure in the record and **no target and no verdict, because decision one of the four says a volume may not read its own mean as a quality figure and no review may certify or fail a volume on one.** The count of paragraphs carrying neither speaker marker, a paragraph carrying neither a quotation mark nor a bold mark, in the first person, that is not inside a speech opened and closed around it: **zero in all ten, published per chapter because publishing zero is cheaper than publishing nothing, and zero only under that instrument — the unrestricted reading that counts every plain narration paragraph returns one hundred and eighty-six, which is correct and is not a fault.** The exchange, a plain speech being a paragraph carrying a quotation mark and no bold mark and answered when the very next non-blank paragraph carries a quotation mark and a bold mark: **sixty plain and thirty-four answered, fifty-six point seven per cent, per chapter 8 of 5, 5 of 3, 5 of 2, 7 of 4, 8 of 4, 9 of 7, 8 of 3, 6 of 3, 2 of 2 and 2 of 1.** The chorus head, every occurrence of the string *about four people in*: **ninety-four in three hundred and ten paragraphs, one in three point three.** Sentences of forty words or more, on a file stripped of its heading line, its `---` rules and its bold marks: **two hundred and eleven, and none stands verbatim in two chapters of the batch.** The longest uninterrupted run of speech paragraphs: **four, and the ceiling holds in all ten.** The longest bolded turn: **seventy-seven words, in Chapter 0352.** Words: **twenty thousand eight hundred and fifty-four, a mean of two thousand and eighty-five.** **Fifty-eight weekday tokens stand across the ten files and every one was read in its own sentence against the day it names, and one fault of the interval family was found in Chapter 0353 and repaired** — a notice count given as three for a room five days away — **and the repair names all three counts in one sentence rather than changing the chapter's argument.**

**WHAT THE BATCH DID NOT DO, and it is the larger half and it is on the record.** Nobody got stronger: Adrian Vale is Stage 2 on all ten days, he performed no working, no threshold was opened, the Narrow Mark is not opened a third time, the other world is not named once, nobody offered him a workway and he asked for none, **the words *passage* and *privilege* are at zero across the ten files**, and he is aged nowhere. **The wall said nothing on all ten days and this batch spends no panel, because the volume's one panel is fixed to day 393 and a day the wall says something on is not a silence.** He is named in **three** of the ten chapters — 0352, 0355 and 0360 — and in no chapter in any form in the other seven, and in all three he is in a room reading a page. The day-154 sentence is not said, restated, paraphrased or improved on. The word that was on the second slate is at zero in all ten. The Crown's schedule was not opened, filled in or withdrawn, and **the word *holder* is at zero across the ten files and is not defined in a mouth.** The two women of twenty-nine were never in one room, **and the arrangement that holds it is on a page in Chapter 0358: the register-keeper stands on the step at the back with no name on it from the tenth hour of the day until the fourth hour of the afternoon and does not come into the room, and Tamsin Quill is at the bench with the folded sheet in her apron from the sixth hour of the morning until the room empties, and the two of them are about four paces apart for as long as she was there and say one word to each other.** **The consent fracture is visible in Chapter 0358 and is not mended in it and nobody apologised in it, and the fifth condition is not given, and the fracture is visible there as a space where a man is not.** The ninth line was not moved, dated, read aloud, taken down, thanked for or resolved. **The Bare Month did not end and no chapter of the ten said it was nearly over or said what it is a month of or printed a day after the forty-fifth, and the words *length*, *long* and *short* applied to the month are at zero.** The town four hundred miles inland is not entered and the page the copy was made from is not fetched. The fence was not joined to the mark and the mark was not measured, dug, counted or probed. **And the count of this city's silences is 251 at day 351 and 260 at day 360, derived, on no page, in no mouth, printed in none of the ten chapters, and for the state layer and not for a chapter.**

**THE FOUR DEBTS CARRY FORWARD WHOLE AND UNPAID, and this batch measured all four on the files and repaired none of them, because a repair pass that quietly takes a routed item to save itself a line is how nineteen quotation marks became a four-volume debt.** Nineteen stray closing quotation marks in Chapters 0281 to 0290; `chapters/volume-07/chapter-0341.md:149`, which reads *the word on the slate has sixteen days left on it* where the figure is one; the forty-word near-duplicate between `chapters/volume-06/chapter-0254.md` and `chapter-0262.md`; and the unmarked first-person paragraph at `chapters/volume-04/chapter-0175.md:47` — line 47, and not `:65` and not `:139`, and both of the wrong numbers are published in the record beside the right one.

# VOLUME 08, BATCH 0001 — REVIEW REPAIR PASS

**This block wins over everything above it. It is the newest block and it is the one a writer reads. The batch block above it is corrected in place and stands, and this block records what the review changed, what it did not, and what it could not touch. Its record is `state/batch-summaries/volume-08-batch-0001.md` and its chapters are `chapters/volume-08/chapter-0351.md` through `chapter-0360.md`. Where this file and a chapter disagree, the chapter is right.**

**WHAT THE REVIEW WAS AND WHAT WAS WRONG WITH IT. Nine findings, and the pass re-derived every figure on its own instrument before acting on any of them, and it took the review's word for none of them.** Three were blocking and three of the three were a record describing work that was not on a page, which is the fault class this repository has been repairing for four volumes: a claim that a thing was done, in a file a later writer inherits as authoritative, where the chapters do not carry it.

1. **THE TWO WOMEN OF TWENTY-NINE WERE IN ONE ROOM IN CHAPTER 0358, IN SPITE OF A PROHIBITION IN THE BATCH'S OWN PROMPT AND IN FOUR FILES THAT SAID THEY NEVER WERE.** The chapter had the register-keeper inside the room from the second hour to the fourth with Tamsin Quill in the room all day, and it printed a look at her. **The prohibition stands and the chapter gave way**, because a batch cannot decide whether these two women are one woman and nothing in this volume settles it. The register-keeper is now on the step at the back with no name on it and does not come in; the two of them are about four paces apart for the whole day and say one word to each other. **Three contradictions inside the chapter fell out of the repair and all three are now true: the line that he asked his question *to a woman who was not in the room*, the line that the two women *have still never once been in the same room*, and a *yesterday* that meant the Sunday, which is day 356 and is now named as that.**
2. **`state/character-state.md` DESCRIBED A SCENE THAT IS NOT ON ANY PAGE.** It reported that the man of about fifty-seven refused a figure out loud, said *do not say it out loud in this room and do not put it on that board*, and gave a reason. **No such speech is in Chapter 0360 or in any of the ten.** The two hundred and fifteen is on a page **in narration, once, with the hundred and forty-fifth named in the same sentence, and the chapter says in its next line that nobody in that room said the figure out loud** — the opposite of what the file claimed. Withdrawn.
3. **THE MAN OF ABOUT THIRTY-NINE'S SPEECH COUNT WAS WRONG ON THE PAGE AND IN THREE FILES.** Chapter 0355's own two closing paragraphs said *four things* and *four sentences* and the chapter prints **two** speeches. The page and the three files now say two.

**AND THE SIX THAT WERE NOT BLOCKING, five of which are figures the next prompt inherits as authoritative and which are therefore corrected in place and not left in a repair note.** The weekday tokens are **fifty-eight**, not seventy-eight; the sentences of forty words or more are **two hundred and eleven**, not one hundred and five, and the published series carried eleven numbers for ten chapters; the per-chapter exchange series was the right series with its first entry moved to the end and a *4 of 4* in front of it, summing to fifty-four against the sixty printed beside it; **day 349 is a Sunday and day 294 is a Monday**, and this file's earlier block had day 349 a Monday and day 294 a Friday of the Short Month; `bible/power-system.md` §46 item 10 is headed *with the definitions beside them* and was carrying two figures without theirs; and `state/continuity.md` said Chapter 0360 names the first two of the four following notices when it names the first and the third, leaving 366 for 370 and 389 for 393 named nowhere.

**AND THE TWO THE REVIEW CALLED RULE AND CRAFT, which are placement and not arithmetic and which were repaired because they are cheap and they are true.** Chapter 0360's second paragraph carried the two hundred and fifteen against its prompt's *no lead paragraph of this batch carries an interval figure at all*; the derivation was clean and the placement was not, and the figure is now in the third paragraph, in the paragraph that says nobody said it out loud. And the *about N seconds* counting device stood twice in Chapter 0356, a chapter whose finding is a refusal, in a batch whose own card bars it twice in one chapter and bars it in a refusal chapter — **the card is self-contradictory and a batch may not amend a card, so the mandated instance in the woman's mouth stands and three narration instances came off the page, and the card's contradiction is recorded at the record's §7 item 4 for the next plan phase to settle.**

**WHAT DID NOT MOVE, and it is the short list and it is the important one. No day, no weekday, no month day, no anchor, no form, no notice, no object count and no plan beat moved. `outline/ending.md` was not opened. No new final enemy was introduced. The seven notices still stand at 351→355, 354→358, 359→363, 362→366, 366→370, 371→375 and 389→393. The volume's own name is still a man's answer spoken once in Chapter 0357 and written down in no file, and this block does not record it. Adrian Vale is still named in three of the ten chapters and in a room reading a page in all three, and 0358 is still a chapter he is not in. The four debts at §5 of the record carry forward unpaid and this pass repaired none of them. The next phase is unchanged and is `workspace/volume-08/batch-0002/PROMPT.md`.**

**THE NEXT PHASE IS `workspace/volume-08/batch-0002/PROMPT.md`, AND IT IS A BATCH AND NOT AN OUTLINE.** It writes Chapters 0361 to 0370, ten chapters, days 361 to 370, from a Friday to a Sunday, one chapter to one day, and it decides nothing, resolves nothing on any live list, invents no person, and records the words spoken in Chapter 0357 in no file it produces. **And the three chapters this pass edited, 0355, 0356 and 0358, are finished prose and are the voice, and a writer may not rewrite them.**

# VOLUME 08, BATCH 0002 — Chapters 0361 to 0370, days 361 to 370

**This block wins over everything above it. It is the authoritative block for the current phase. Its record is `state/batch-summaries/volume-08-batch-0002.md` and its ten chapters are on disk. The block it displaces is the Batch 0001 review repair pass, and `state/volume-07-close.md` is Volume 07's inheritance and is not displaced, and `outline/volume-08.md` is still the plan and where a chapter and the plan disagree the chapter is right.**

**THE CALENDAR HELD, and it was rebuilt from day 1 being a Tuesday and from nothing else before any prose was written and then checked row by row against the plan and the prompt: days 361 to 370, one chapter to one day, no double day, the form *the Nth day of the Bare Month*, the Nth being day less 315. This batch: 361 Friday (the forty-sixth), 362 Saturday, 363 Sunday, 364 Monday, 365 Tuesday, 366 Wednesday (the fifty-first), 367 Thursday, 368 Friday, 369 Saturday, 370 Sunday (the fifty-fifth). Thirty weekday tokens stand across the ten files and every one was read in the sentence it stands in against the day that sentence names, and six were taken out as unplaceable and replaced with a day of the Bare Month in the same sentence, and the record names all six.**

**WHAT IS ON A PAGE NOW, and it is ten chapters and it is finished prose and it is not a plan.** **A man who walks a four-hundred-mile road with a barrow and has walked it four times in nine years is on a page for the first time in this manuscript, in Chapter 0361, and his second handle is invented there — a hurdle he made and a bar he replaced — and in Chapter 0370 he is in a room over a market in daylight with a date on the door, and nobody asked him about the gate.** Three of the four who have not left were in one room on the forty-eighth day of that month and a woman of about twenty-one was asked which of the four places under a heading is a person and answered **none of the four, the second one counts them**, and nobody in that room marked the moment. A man of about fifty-seven counted sixteen posts and eleven withies out loud on a shut road and a man of about sixty with a handcart heard him do it and neither asked the other one question. A wall with the shape of nine boards on it and about four inches of dry stone at the foot of it is bare and the shape is going off it, and a man with a piece of chalk he does not use stood in front of it and did not write on it. A man of about fifty-two who digs was asked a task on the fifty-first day of that month and said yes with a condition stated first — he will not put a hand on it, he will not put a rod in it, and he will not leave a mark of any kind on it — and the room accepted it in about four seconds. A woman of about twenty-one's pen was found out to have been ground by the man of about forty-eight nine feet away from her, who admitted it in one sentence. **A share with the post taken out was said out loud at exactly two hundred days, measured from the thirteenth day of the Long Month, which is the hundred and sixty-eighth day of this flood, and both anchors are in the same sentence in a mouth, and the figure is the one round figure that falls inside this batch, and the paper is in the inside of a coat belonging to a man in this city who is not on those boards and is in no chapter of these ten in any form, and the man who said it did not say how he knows and nobody asked him, and the coat is not opened.** About four hundred and forty people on eleven miles of flats make a salt that has not come off a wharf in a cart since before the water, and a room found that out and sent nothing. And on the fifty-fifth day of that month a room spent a Sunday doing the half of a task that is not its own and agreed, out loud, in about four seconds, not to send for the man it is waiting for, and a man who has never been in a room in this city said the reason and it was about the price of a thing being findable.

**THE FIGURES, and the definitions travel with them, and the record is where they are measured.** The share of words inside bold marks, heading line included, `---` rules counted, bold marks counted as nothing, file split on whitespace: **5.93, 11.41, 7.27, 12.46, 22.18, 11.81, 19.51, 11.26, 24.23 and 8.87 per cent, a range of 5.9 to 24.2 and a mean of 13.5 unweighted and 13.4 weighted by words, with each chapter's subject beside the figure in the record and no target and no verdict, because decision one of the four says a volume may not read its own mean as a quality figure and no review may certify or fail a volume on one. THIS LIST IS THE MEASURED ONE AND THE TWO LISTS THIS BLOCK PUBLISHED — 6.0 to 24.9 with a mean of 13.7, and before that 6.2 to 22.8 with a mean of 12.9 — DID NOT REPRODUCE ON THIS INSTRUMENT, AND THE COUNT OF CHAPTERS THAT MOVED BETWEEN THEM IS SEVEN AND NOT FIVE; THE FIGURES THEY REPLACE ARE NAMED BESIDE THEM IN THE RECORD AT ITS §3 ITEM 1.** The longest bolded turn is **eighty-five words, in Chapter 0367**, under the working ceiling of about ninety and under the hard one of ninety-five. The count of paragraphs carrying neither speaker marker, a paragraph carrying neither a quotation mark nor a bold mark, in the first person, that is not inside a speech opened and closed around it: **zero in all ten, published per chapter because publishing zero is cheaper than publishing nothing.** The exchange, a plain speech being a paragraph BEGINNING with a quotation mark and carrying no bold mark and answered when the very next non-blank paragraph also begins with a quotation mark and carries a bold mark — **and that definition is the corrected one, because the looser instrument this block first published counted a narration paragraph containing a quotation mark as a speech and its figure did not reproduce: fifty-five plain and forty answered, seventy-two point seven per cent, per chapter 5 of 2, 5 of 4, 3 of 3, 5 of 3, 8 of 7, 5 of 2, 6 of 4, 4 of 3, 9 of 7 and 5 of 5, and the figure the looser instrument gave was fifty-three of thirty-nine and it is withdrawn.** The chorus head, **on two instruments, because the first was too narrow and the conclusion built on it was an artefact of the narrowness** — on the declared string *about four people in*, **sixty-seven in two hundred and fifty-one paragraphs, one in three point seven, at zero in four of the ten (0361, 0364, 0365, 0368)**, and on every variant of the frame, *about four people* plus any following word, **one hundred and thirty in the same 251 paragraphs, one in one point nine, with no chapter at zero**. Sentences of forty words or more: **one hundred and seventy-four, and none stands verbatim in more than one chapter of the batch.** The longest uninterrupted run of speech paragraphs, on the corrected speech-paragraph definition: **four, and it holds at four in six of the ten and the ceiling is not exceeded in any.** Markdown bold parity is zero in all ten. Words: **eighteen thousand two hundred and ninety-nine.** The word-run across the ten returns a top of twenty-five, **and it was run twice, with the lead paragraphs subtracted and not subtracted, because the subtraction was hiding a forty-word run in three lead paragraphs — see the record's §10 item 14.**

**WHAT THE BATCH DID NOT DO, and it is the larger half and it is on the record.** Nobody got stronger: Adrian Vale is Stage 2 on all ten days, he is in **no chapter of this batch in any form**, he performed no working, no threshold was opened, the Narrow Mark is not opened a third time, the other world is not named once, nobody offered him a workway and he asked for none, **the words *passage* and *privilege* are at zero across the ten files**, and he is aged nowhere. **The wall said nothing on all ten days and this batch spends no panel, because the volume's one panel is fixed to day 393 and a day the wall says something on is not a silence.** Nobody was asked whether anything will go, and the room in which somebody is asked is the room on the sixtieth day of that month, which is five chapters past the end of this batch, and **no chapter of these ten wrote the notice for it and it went out on the thirty-first day of the Bare Month, which is day 371, and it is not written here.** **The two notices this batch carried went out on day 362 for the room on day 366 and on day 366 for the room on day 370, both four days, both unsigned, both with nothing at the top of either, both to a place and not to a house, and no name was added to either and there is no eighth notice.** No chapter counted the days since the woman of about sixty-one said no, at any value, in narration or in a mouth. The day-154 sentence is not said, restated, paraphrased or improved on. The word that was on the second slate is at zero in all ten. The Crown's schedule was not opened, filled in or withdrawn, and **the word *holder* is at zero across the ten files and is not defined in a mouth.** The two women of twenty-nine were never in one room and neither of them is in any of these ten chapters, and **the consent fracture is not mended in any of them and nobody apologised and the fifth condition is not given.** The ninth line was not moved, dated, read aloud, taken down, thanked for or resolved. **The Bare Month did not end and no chapter of the ten said it was nearly over or said what it is a month of or printed a day after the fifty-fifth, and the words *length*, *long* and *short* applied to the month are at zero.** The town four hundred miles inland is not entered and the page the copy was made from is not fetched and the eight names at the foot of that page are not read. The fence was not joined to the mark, no distance between them is printed, and the mark was not measured, dug, counted, probed or resolved and no rod went into anything. **And the count of this city's silences is 261 at day 361 and 270 at day 370, derived, on no page, in no mouth, printed in none of the ten chapters, and for the state layer and not for a chapter.**

**AND THE ONE THING A LATER WRITER HAS TO KNOW BECAUSE IT WAS WRONG IN A FILE IT INHERITED.** `state/character-state.md` and the prompt for this batch both said that Chapter 0360 named a man of about fifty-nine as a fact about what nobody in this city has a page for. **It does not. The string returns nothing across the ten files of Chapter 0351 to 0360, and no chapter of Volume 07 names him at all, and the claim is withdrawn and corrected here and in `state/character-state.md`.** Chapter 0361 is the chapter that puts him on a page, which is what `outline/volume-08.md` §20.8 always said it would be, and his second handle is **a hurdle he made and a bar he replaced** and it is the same in every chapter he is in after it. **And one wording departed from the plan and is recorded at the record's §6: the plan's row for him says *in the ninth year of this flood*, and day 370 is the three hundred and seventieth day of this flood, so no chapter of this volume can print a ninth year of it, and the two chapters print nine years, which is the figure the plan itself gives him.**

**THE FOUR DEBTS CARRY FORWARD WHOLE AND UNPAID, and this batch re-measured all four on the files and repaired none of them.** Nineteen stray closing quotation marks in Chapters 0281 to 0290; `chapters/volume-07/chapter-0341.md:149`, which reads *the word on the slate has sixteen days left on it* where the figure is one; the forty-word near-duplicate between `chapters/volume-06/chapter-0254.md` and `chapter-0262.md`; and the unmarked first-person paragraph at `chapters/volume-04/chapter-0175.md:47` — line 47, and not `:65` and not `:139`.

**1. A STATE FILE AND THE BATCH RECORD BOTH SAID FOUR FIGURES WERE NINE AND THEN TEN, AND THREE OF THEM WERE.** `state/continuity.md`'s newest block and the record's §10 item 4 both claimed that all four room counts of the man of about fifty-seven stood at nine and then ten. `chapter-0364.md:7` said ten and `:39` said seven, seven lines apart, in the same chapter, the same man, the same fortnight, and `chapter-0363.md:45` had nine then ten. **Line 39 now reads ten.** **A record that says a thing was done is worth nothing unless it was, and this is the same fault the volume has now been bitten by twice, which is why it is named here instead of being quietly fixed.**

**2. THE *ABOUT TWO HUNDRED ROOMS* FIGURE WAS ON THREE MEN IN THE TEN CHAPTERS OF THIS BATCH AND IS NOW ON TWO.** **It is not one man's private number.** `state/character-state.md` and `state/open-threads.md` both give the **man of about thirty-one** about two hundred rooms and about eleven kitchens a month and the distinction that he is the only person in nine kitchens nobody asked anything in, which is on a page at `chapters/volume-07/chapter-0343.md:29` in another form. The **man of about forty-eight** has it in his own mouth at Chapter 0367 and at `chapter-0352.md`. **Chapter 0368 gave the man of about thirty-nine both that room count and the man of about forty-eight's trade claim, *has never once repeated a thing he heard in one*, which is the sentence that man says and calls a trade; the man of about thirty-nine has no room count on any page and now has none.** **Chapter 0369 and Chapter 0370 each gave the man of about thirty-one a predicate that is not his — *has not been asked one question in about two hundred rooms*, when his own figure is about two hundred rooms and about eleven kitchens and nine of the eleven — and Chapter 0370 left the observer of a new man walking into a room unstated, so the only man in the room carrying a room count would have been the man of about fifty-nine, who had never been in a room in this city and who is the subject of the sentence.** **All four are on their own pages now, and the string stands four times in these ten files and nowhere else in the volume: `chapter-0367.md:35` and `:61` in the man of about forty-eight's own mouth, `chapter-0367.md:37` in the narration about him, and `chapter-0369.md:13` in the narration about the man of about thirty-one.** **AND A THIRD HOLDER EXISTS AND IS NOT IN THIS BATCH'S TEN CHAPTERS, AND THE FIGURE IS THEREFORE ON THREE MEN IN THIS VOLUME AND NOT ON TWO: `chapters/volume-08/chapter-0360.md:45` gives it to Adrian Vale, in *the shortest true thing he has said in about two hundred rooms in this city*, which is Batch 0001's file, which this pass did not edit, and which no review of this batch raised. **3. Current phase: **volume-08-batch-0003 (COMPLETE. TEN CHAPTERS ON DISK: Chapters 0371 to 0380, days 371 to 380, one chapter to one day, from a Monday to a Wednesday, the Bare Month days fifty-six to sixty-five, the ordinal being the day less 315. `bible/power-system.md` §48 EXISTS, `state/batch-summaries/volume-08-batch-0003.md` is written, and ten entries stand at the foot of `state/chapter-summaries.md`. NO DECISION MOVED: no day, no weekday, no month day, no anchor, no live thread's state, no card, no outline, and none of the eight decisions of record was altered, and §§32, 33, 33A and 33B were not amended. THE NEXT PHASE IS `workspace/volume-08/batch-0004/PROMPT.md`, AND IT IS A BATCH.)**

**THE RUN WAS BUILT FROM DAY 1 BEING A TUESDAY AND FROM NOTHING ELSE before any prose was written, and then checked against the batch prompt's table row by row, and all ten rows agree: 371 Monday, 372 Tuesday, 373 Wednesday, 374 Thursday, 375 Friday, 376 Saturday, 377 Sunday, 378 Monday, 379 Tuesday, 380 Wednesday. There is no double day in this batch and there is no double day in this volume.**

**THE TEN DAYS IN ONE LINE. A notice went out for the room on the sixtieth day of the Bare Month, four days, unsigned, with nothing at the top of it, to a place and not to a house, and its own morning and the room's day were named in one sentence with the count. A man who has put a number in a column four times a year since the winter before the water said out loud that he has never once filled in the fourth of four places and has never gone to a household to find out how you fill one in, and nobody asked him about it. A woman unfolded the only page in this city that has anything to do with a road, in the open, and folded it again the same way, and a crease in it has gone soft. A room arranged the length of a bench and decided out loud not to say what it is for. On the sixtieth day, in daylight, with a date in chalk on the door, Tamsin Quill asked the woman of about twenty-one whether it will go, and nobody in that room asked her a second question and nobody after that morning is going to be the second one. A man worked out in a yard the order nine things are done in and a woman of about sixty-nine would not tell him which of the nine he had wrong. A roof came down by hand in a squall and a number went back to the woman who owns it. Eight places across two documents in one room were counted out loud and not one of the eight is the same as any of the other seven, and the two were not put side by side. Nine rows at the back of a register that nobody can date were looked at and were not copied. A sheet with about nine places on it went onto a bench in a room with about nine people in it and a woman said out loud that those are two nines and they are not the same nine, and that the sheet is not going to anybody. Nothing was measured, dug, counted, probed or resolved, and no character in any of the ten chapters goes and looks at the line in the ground.**

**AND TWO FIGURES IN THE INHERITED RECORD THAT WERE WRONG, CORRECTED IN PLACE, AND THE CORRECTION IS THE POINT OF THE SECOND ONE.** **THE PROMPT THIS BATCH INHERITED SAYS THE NOTICE IN CHAPTER 0371 IS THE SEVENTH OF THE VOLUME'S SEVEN AND IT IS THE SIXTH.** The register of the seven is on the page four times and says 351, 354, 359, 362, 366, 371 and 389 for rooms on 355, 358, 363, 366, 370, 375 and 393 — `bible/power-system.md` §33A item 7, §47 item 5, `outline/volume-08.md` §14.4 and §20.10, and `state/batch-summaries/volume-08-batch-0002.md` §11. **Chapter 0371 carries the sixth, the prompt's own object table names the same seven days, the seventh goes out on day 389 for the room on day 393, and no chapter of these ten says seventh of anything.** A batch that had written it would have produced a chapter with every figure in it correct and a count of the volume's instruments in it that is wrong by one.

**AND `outline/volume-08.md` CONTRADICTS ITSELF ABOUT ADRIAN VALE IN THE MIDPOINT, and the chapter followed §4 and not §8.** §8 ends *Adrian is in that room and asks nothing and is asked nothing and is on a page because he is reading the second slate*; **§4 gives the full list of the chapters of this volume he is in — 0352, 0355, 0360, 0371, 0376, 0381 and 0392 — and 0375 is not on it, and the batch prompt makes §4 binding.** Chapter 0375 has no Adrian in it in any form, and the second slate in that chapter is on a shelf behind a table with a date on it and nothing under it, and nobody in that room goes near it.

**THE THINGS THAT ARE NOW ON A PAGE, AND EVERY ONE OF THEM IS A GAIN AND NONE OF THEM IS A POWER.** A notice with the price of it said out loud by the woman who writes them. A man saying out loud, in a room, that he has never filled in one of four places in about nine years and has never gone to find out how. A room deciding not to say what it is for. A question asked once and not asked twice. The order nine things are done in behind a row of kitchens, and a woman who would not say which of the nine a man had wrong, and why. Eight places across two documents and not one of them the same as another, and a word nobody in a city has said out loud in a room in nine years said out loud by not being said. Nine rows nobody can date, and a room's decision not to copy them, and her reason. A crease in a sheet of road that has gone soft. Two nines in one room that are not the same nine. **None of them is a power. One of them is the price of a power. All of them are heavier than a power.**

**AND THE HOUSE FIGURES, MEASURED AND NOT WRITTEN, WITH THE DEFINITIONS BESIDE THEM.** The share of words inside bold marks, heading line included, `---` rules counted, bold marks counted as nothing, file split on whitespace: **10.91, 7.98, 10.64, 14.80, 8.30, 6.26, 4.48, 17.58, 8.23 and 12.11 per cent, a range of 4.48 to 17.58, a mean of 10.13 unweighted and 10.17 weighted by words on 17,543 words, and no target and no verdict is set on any of them.** The longest bolded turn in the ten is **eighty-five words**, against the working ceiling of about ninety and the hard one of ninety-five. The longest run of consecutive speech paragraphs is **two, in all ten**. Long sentences of forty words or more: **143, none duplicated**. The count of paragraphs carrying neither speaker marker: **zero in all ten.** The exchange, twenty plain speeches and twenty answered, seventy-six point nine per cent. **The chorus frame on the bare head *about four people in* is seventy in one hundred and ninety-eight paragraphs, one in 2.8, at zero in two of the ten, and on the variant-agnostic instrument *about four people* plus any word it is one hundred and five in the same one hundred and ninety-eight, one in 1.9, with no chapter at zero — and Batch 0001, Batch 0002 and this batch stand at one in 2.0, one in 1.9 and one in 1.9 on that instrument, and the three are published and the trend is not claimed.**

**ADRIAN IS NAMED IN TWO OF THE TEN AND IN THE OTHER EIGHT HE IS IN NO CHAPTER IN ANY FORM, and the two are 0371 and 0376, and the plan's own §4 list is what that follows.** In 0371 he is in a room reading a page: a sheet folded into four with a date on the back and two names on the front of it, taken out of the inside of his own coat and put on a table about nine feet from a sheet that had just gone out with nothing at the top of it, and neither fact was said out loud and the coat was buttoned again. # VOLUME 08, BATCH 0003 — REVIEW REPAIR PASS — the authoritative block, and it wins over every block above it

**A review of this batch raised eight findings. The prose of all ten chapters was reworked, the state layer that reported a defect as a flat trend was corrected, the plan's contradiction about the protagonist was corrected in the plan, and the next phase's prompt was repaired. No chapter was restarted, no plot, no beat, no day, no weekday, no anchor, no cast member and no decision of record moved, and no controller file was touched.** The disposition is `reviews/volume-08/batch-0003.md`; the batch's own half is `state/batch-summaries/volume-08-batch-0003.md` §11.

**WHAT WAS REPAIRED, AND IT IS THE FINDING THE REVIEW CALLED BLOCKING AND WAS RIGHT TO.** **The narration voice in Chapters 0371 to 0380 had collapsed into a single repeated sentence frame that restated the sentence immediately before it, at one instance in every 1.9 paragraphs with no chapter at zero, running sixteen consecutive times in Chapter 0371 and twenty-three times in Chapter 0375, three of them inside one paragraph.** That fails three quality-gate rules on their face — *the prose reads as natural fiction rather than an outline*, *every chapter changes the situation*, and the AGENTS.md rule against words used as mechanical filler. **All 133 occurrences of the frame's clause-head and 105 of the frame itself across the ten files were read in their paragraphs and rewritten sentence by sentence, on the method `outline/volume-08.md` §17 item 11 already names: delete the string and its clause-head and re-attach the observation to the sentence it was standing in, which cannot be done by substitution.** **A great deal of real content in this batch was sitting inside the frame and the frame was the only reason it read as filler; the observations were kept, including the new ones, and what was cut was the sentence that said the observation had been made by somebody.**

**THE REPAIRED MEASUREMENTS, WITH THE DEFINITIONS, AND THE FIRST EDITIONS BESIDE THEM BECAUSE A SILENT CORRECTION IS THE THIRD VERSION OF THE SAME FAULT.** The chorus frame on the variant-agnostic instrument, *about four people* followed by any word: **one hundred and five in one hundred and ninety-eight paragraphs, one in 1.9, before; thirty-two in one hundred and ninety-eight, one in 6.2, after.** On the declared head *about four people in*: **seventy in one hundred and ninety-eight, one in 2.8, before; fifteen in one hundred and ninety-eight, one in 13.2, after.** The share of words inside bold marks, heading line included, `---` rules counted, bold marks counted as nothing, file split on whitespace: **12.24, 9.10, 11.92, 16.52, 9.57, 7.05, 5.11, 19.96, 9.20 and 13.15 per cent after**, a range of 5.11 to 19.96, a mean of 11.38 unweighted and 11.47 weighted by words on 15,837 words, against the first edition's **10.91, 7.98, 10.64, 14.80, 8.30, 6.26, 4.48, 17.58, 8.23 and 12.11 on 17,543 words** — **four of the ten moved up and none moved down, because the repair cut narration and cut no speech, and no target and no verdict is set on any of them.** The longest bolded turn in the ten is **eighty-five words**, unchanged, against the working ceiling of about ninety and the hard one of ninety-five. The longest run of consecutive speech paragraphs is **two in nine of the ten and three in Chapter 0379**, within the ceiling of four. The count of paragraphs carrying neither speaker marker: **zero in all ten.** The highest word-run involving any of the ten files is **thirty-three words, between Chapters 0374 and 0378**, which is the volume's room-lead frame and is the same figure the batch published. **Markdown bold parity is zero unbalanced in all ten, and the double comma at `chapter-0380.md:37` is repaired.**

**AND WHAT DID NOT MOVE, WHICH IS THE HALF OF A REPAIR PASS THAT MATTERS MOST.** **The set of bolded speech paragraphs in all ten files is byte-identical to the first edition** — every line of dialogue, every speaker mark and every line break inside the marks, verified by extraction and set comparison. **The multiset of every digit string across the ten files is unchanged: zero lost and zero added.** **No weekday token and no Bare Month day was added, changed or removed, and the only difference is a plural — *three Mondays in a row* became *the third Monday in a row*, same day, same fact.** No notice, no form, no object count, no descriptor, no second handle, no anchor, no day, no card and no thread's state changed. The day map stands as published: 371 Monday, 372 Tuesday, 373 Wednesday, 374 Thursday, 375 Friday, 376 Saturday, 377 Sunday, 378 Monday, 379 Tuesday, 380 Wednesday.

**AND THE THREE FINDINGS THAT ARE CARRIED AND NOT REPAIRED, because each is a decision a batch may not make, and none of them is dismissed.** **The room is the lead in seven of ten chapters and the volume's first thirty contain one room in nine — new Debt 6, for the Volume 08 close. Adrian Vale is in two of the ten and five of the thirty, and `outline/volume-08.md` §4 names every chapter of the volume he is in and the batch honoured that list exactly — new Debt 8, for the Volume 08 close, and the review is right that it is thin. The cast is told apart by the object in each person's hand, and the descriptor map and §17 item 9 both require a second handle and the requirement was not enough — new Debt 7, for an outline or a close.** The three are in `state/open-threads.md` in full and in `workspace/volume-08/batch-0004/PROMPT.md` under *Two findings the review of the last batch raised that a batch may not fix*.

**AND THE STATE LAYER WAS ITSELF PART OF THE FAULT, WHICH IS THE FINDING NO REVIEW OF A PROSE PASS HAS EVER PUT THIS WAY.** **This block's predecessor reported the frame at one in 1.9 and printed the sentence *this record reports that as a measurement and not as a result* in the same breath as the mechanism that made the number meaningless.** The next prompt then called the same ten chapters *the voice* and told the next writer they might not be rewritten, so a defect was on its way to becoming a house standard. **A defect that is measured, named, published and inherited is still a defect, and the finding is published in the batch record rather than deleted from it, because a record that quietly rewrites itself is the failure this repository names.** `outline/volume-08.md` §17 item 11 now carries an item 11a that is a rule about sentences and not about numbers, and `workspace/volume-08/batch-0004/PROMPT.md` binds it, publishes both editions of the bolded share, and no longer tells a writer to copy a defective habit uncritically.

**AND `state/phase-ledger.json` STILL READS `phase-000-bootstrap / planned / attempts: 0` AND WAS NOT TOUCHED.** It is controller-owned, it is named in every phase prompt in this repository as not a phase's to edit, and it is recorded here the way every prior review recorded it.

**THE NEXT PHASE IS `workspace/volume-08/batch-0004/PROMPT.md` AND IT IS A BATCH AND ITS RANGE IS UNCHANGED.** It writes Chapters 0381 to 0390, days 381 to 390, from a Thursday to a Saturday, and it is told that day 385 is a trap day and that the thirty-nine men under the near gate are exactly three hundred days unpaid on it measured from the eighty-fifth, and that day 389 is the seventh and last notice for the room on day 393, and that day 391 is when three people go down the road with a barrow each.

## VOLUME 08, BATCH 0003 — THE STATE-LAYER COMPACTION, and it is the first time in this repository that anything in the state layer has been MOVED rather than appended to

**A review of this batch raised the state files as a MEDIUM finding and it was right: `state/continuity.md` stood at 1.1 MB, `state/character-state.md` at 712 KB, `state/chapter-summaries.md` at 644 KB and `state/open-threads.md` at 496 KB, every one of them an append-only archive in which each batch restated in full what it had done, and the next writer was instructed to read roughly two megabytes of prior context before writing a word. AGENTS.md asks for rolling summaries. These were not rolling summaries.**

**WHAT WAS DONE.** **The body of each of the five large state files that belongs to Volumes 01 to 06 was moved to `state/archive/`, verbatim, and each archive carries in its own header why it moved, what is true of it, how many lines went, and the SHA256 of the moved text so that a later pass can prove it did not alter what it moved.** Nothing was edited, abbreviated, corrected, summarised away or deleted. **What is live in each file is the whole of Volume 07 and the whole of Volume 08 to date, which is one complete volume and one in progress, and that is a rolling window rather than an archive.**

| File | Before | After | Moved to |
|---|---|---|---|
| `state/current.md` | 323 KB | **95 KB** | `state/archive/current.md.volumes-01-to-06.md` |
| `state/continuity.md` | 1,072 KB | **204 KB** | `state/archive/continuity.md.volumes-01-to-06.md` |
| `state/character-state.md` | 711 KB | **260 KB** | `state/archive/character-state.md.volumes-01-to-06.md` |
| `state/chapter-summaries.md` | 642 KB | **148 KB** | `state/archive/chapter-summaries.md.volumes-01-to-06.md` |
| `state/open-threads.md` | 498 KB | **152 KB** | `state/archive/open-threads.md.volumes-01-to-06.md` |
| **live total** | **3,246 KB** | **859 KB** | archive 2,387 KB, retained whole |

**THE CHECK, RUN AFTER THE LAST EDIT AND NOT BEFORE, AND IT IS REPRODUCIBLE FROM GIT.** Every non-blank, non-heading line of all five files at `HEAD` was compared against the union of the live file and its archive: **zero lines lost in all five, and zero headings lost in all five.** The character roster at the head of `state/character-state.md` and the file headers of the other four were kept in place, and `state/current.md`, which had no file-level header left after the cut, was given one, because **its first line had been naming `volume-08-batch-0001` as the current phase and that phase ran three batches ago** — a stale navigation line of exactly the class every prior review in this repository has raised.

**AND WHY IT IS RECORDED AT LENGTH INSTEAD OF BEING DONE QUIETLY.** **A record that quietly rewrites itself is the failure this repository names, and this is the largest rewrite of the state layer since the manuscript began.** So: the cut is stated, the reason is stated, the table is published, the archives are retained whole, and the checks that prove nothing was lost are named so a later phase can rerun them. **A writer who needs Volumes 01 to 06 is not deprived of them; they are in one directory and it is called `archive`.**

**AND WHAT WAS DELIBERATELY NOT COMPACTED.** `state/volume-01-close.md` through `state/volume-07-close.md` and all of `state/batch-summaries/` are untouched, because a close record is a decision of record and a batch record is the witness of a phase, and neither is a rolling file. **`state/phase-ledger.json` was not touched and never may be; it still reads `phase-000-bootstrap / planned / attempts: 0` and every review in this repository has recorded that and none of them has edited it.**
