The mean is a consequence and not a plan.** The count of paragraphs carrying neither speaker marker is **zero in all ten** on the first-person instrument, and a paragraph carrying a bold mark and no quotation mark is **zero in all ten**, and no exchange carries two bold marks. **93 narration paragraphs and 56 speech paragraphs make the 149.** The exchange is **twenty plain speeches and twenty answered, one hundred per cent**, and the cause is published and is not a target. The longest bolded turn is **seventy-seven words, twice**, and no turn is over ninety and none over the hard ceiling of ninety-five, **and five turns were over the hard ceiling on a first pass and every one was broken by a plain-speech interjection and a narration beat and NO TURN WAS CUT TO MAKE A NUMBER.** Long sentences of forty words or more: **65 on reading A and 65 on reading B, and the two readings RETURN THE SAME FIGURE IN ALL TEN OF THESE FILES, and the reason is published and is that no paragraph in these ten files carries more than two quotation marks**, on a batch mean of **27.5** words to the sentence on both readings, longest single sentence 74. **THE LONGEST RUN OF SPEECH PARAGRAPHS IS TWO AND NOT FOUR.** The paragraph composition, published as four separate counts and never as their sum: **fifty-six speech, twenty-five one-sentence narration lead-ins, seven free-standing one-sentence narration paragraphs, and forty-seven of three or more sentences**, and a paragraph of three or more sentences carrying a physical action is at least one in every one of the ten, at 6, 5, 4, 4, 4, 4, 5, 5, 4 and 4 against a whole of 6, 5, 5, 5, 4, 4, 5, 5, 4 and 4, **and the subset EQUALS the whole in two of the ten and is smaller in eight.**

**THE WORD-RUN CHECK AT BOTH THRESHOLDS. At forty: ZERO, within the ten and against all four hundred and eighty-nine pre-existing files. At sixteen: ZERO, within the ten, and ZERO against the four hundred and eighty-nine pre-existing files, and the twenty-two sixteen-word clones the check found before the rewrites are published as twenty-two rows in the batch record at §7, and NO FACT, DAY, COUNT, OBJECT, CAST PLACEMENT OR PROHIBITION MOVED IN ANY OF THEM.**

### 4. THE THINGS THAT CAME BACK NON-ZERO, WHICH ARE PUBLISHED AND NOT PASSED

***level* IS AT ONE, in Chapter 0491, in the sentence that says water finds its own level along the flags, and the volume's own rule makes the exception for an ordinary English word about water and asks for the figure.** *rooms* is at one in Chapter 0493 and is a general reference and not a room count for anybody. *notice* and *notices* are at ZERO in all ten, and the card said one of them would stand, and the departure is published. *box* is at one and it is the store's own chalk box on the step, and the shut box of the flood is in none of the ten. *fence* is at one and it stands along the top of the shut road with no post and no withy and no total. **AND THE INHERITED ONE: *sledge* is at ZERO across these ten files on both readings, and the close's list of fifty-six zeros still says it is at zero across four hundred and fifty files when it stands at seven in two of them, and that error is not this batch's to repair.**

### 5. What this batch did not do

**No chapter of the ten carries a panel and the wall said nothing on all ten. No chapter writes a notice. No chapter traces, investigates or guesses at the mark that came back out of place, and the sheets of day 438 and day 440 are named nowhere. The man of about fifty-seven is in none of the ten and the length of new rope is named in none of them and is not used, cut, taken up or dropped. The party of four is in none of the ten and no elapsed count of days stands in any of them except the two the plan requires, and both name their anchors in the same sentence. The three questions are not restated, improved on or answered, and no chapter says they rhyme. The fourth question is asked once, to one man, in Chapter 0498, and he does not answer it. The day-154 sentence is not said, restated, paraphrased or improved on, and the count of chapters carrying the four-second beat is not published. No descriptor was taken for anybody and no person was invented; the pool is empty measured on all four hundred and fifty files. The woman who walked a market is in one chapter of the ten, is asked nothing, and is given no name, no number and no descriptor. No ordinal for a month and no day after the hundred and eighty-fifth. Nobody gets stronger. NO CARD WAS EDITED AFTER ITS CHAPTER WAS WRITTEN, nothing under `outline/` was touched, no day moved, and `chapters/volume-10/chapter-0475.md` is still not on disk.**
